COLDWAR_FRA2_migrate_starting_spirit_revision = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA2_is_1970 = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_starting_spirit_revision_20261029_migrated }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_executive
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_parliament
				}
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_civic_settlement }
				NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_institutional_balance_residual }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_presidency
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_presidency
					add_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_institutional_balance_residual
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_institutional_balance_residual
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_civic_settlement
				has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_institutional_balance_residual
			}
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_institutional_balance_residual
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_technical_colleges
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_mass_universities
				}
				NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_higher_education_pressures }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_education
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_education
					add_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_higher_education_pressures
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_higher_education_pressures
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_oil_resilience
				}
				NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_oil_dependence_residual }
			}
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_oil
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_oil_dependence_intermediate
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_oil_dependence_residual
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_nuclear_scale
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_diverse_energy
				}
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_oil_resilience }
				NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_oil_dependence_intermediate }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_oil
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_oil
					add_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_oil_dependence_intermediate
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_oil_dependence_intermediate
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_starting_spirit_revision_20261029_migrated
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_add_domestic_weekly_expenses = {

}

COLDWAR_FRA2_complete_public_service = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA2_is_1970 = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_public_service }
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.00080 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_institutions_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier = -0.03 tooltip = bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_public_service
		COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_complete_executive = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA2_is_1970 = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_executive }
			has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_presidency
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_public_service
			}
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_parliament }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_presidency
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_presidency
				add_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_institutional_balance_residual
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_institutions_political_power_factor = 0.12 tooltip = political_power_factor_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_institutions_stability_factor = -0.02 tooltip = stability_factor_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_institutions_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier = 0.03 tooltip = bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_recovery_applied_executive
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_executive
		COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_complete_parliament = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA2_is_1970 = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_parliament }
			has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_presidency

			}
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_executive }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_presidency
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_presidency
				add_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_institutional_balance_residual
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_institutions_stability_factor = 0.05 tooltip = stability_factor_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_institutions_political_power_factor = -0.05 tooltip = political_power_factor_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_institutions_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier = -0.03 tooltip = bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_recovery_applied_parliament
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_parliament
		COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_build_contract_state_57_state_offices = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_contract_state_57_state_offices }
			owns_state = 57
			controls_state = 57
			has_full_control_of_state = 57
			57 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@offices value = 49 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		57 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			if = {
				limit = {
					free_building_slots = {
						building = offices
						size > 0
						include_locked = no
					}
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
				ROOT = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_contract_state_57_state_offices }
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_complete_contract = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA2_is_1970 = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_contract }
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_executive
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_parliament
			}
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_statute }
			COLDWAR_FRA2_assets_ready_contract = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.00300 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_grenelle
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 10 }
		change_labour_unions_opinion = yes
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -5 }
		change_industrial_conglomerates_opinion = yes
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_institutions_social_cost_multiplier_modifier = 0.08 tooltip = social_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_build_contract_state_57_state_offices = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_contract
		COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_new_society }
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_progress
			}
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_presidential_industry }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.00200 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_tech_bonus = {
			name = COLDWAR_FRA2_new_society_research
			bonus = 0.5
			uses = 1
			category = CAT_industry
		}
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 10 }
		change_labour_unions_opinion = yes
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_udr_education_cost_multiplier_modifier = 0.06 tooltip = education_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_udr_political_power_factor = -0.04 tooltip = political_power_factor_tt }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_new_society
		COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_build_presidential_industry_state_55_state_industrial_complex = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_presidential_industry_state_55_state_industrial_complex }
			owns_state = 55
			controls_state = 55
			has_full_control_of_state = 55
			55 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@industrial_complex value = 49 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		55 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			if = {
				limit = {
					free_building_slots = {
						building = industrial_complex
						size > 0
						include_locked = no
					}
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = industrial_complex
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
				ROOT = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_presidential_industry_state_55_state_industrial_complex }
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_build_regional_councils_state_64_state_infrastructure = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_regional_councils_state_64_state_infrastructure }
			owns_state = 64
			controls_state = 64
			has_full_control_of_state = 64
			64 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		64 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = infrastructure
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
			ROOT = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_regional_councils_state_64_state_infrastructure }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_complete_regional_councils = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA2_is_1970 = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_regional_councils }
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			neutrality_neutral_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_social_market
			}
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_cdp_compromise }
			COLDWAR_FRA2_assets_ready_regional_councils = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.00300 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		COLDWAR_FRA2_build_regional_councils_state_64_state_infrastructure = yes
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_centre_political_power_factor = -0.03 tooltip = political_power_factor_tt }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
		change_farmers_opinion = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_regional_councils
		COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_build_mutuals_state_56_state_offices = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_mutuals_state_56_state_offices }
			owns_state = 56
			controls_state = 56
			has_full_control_of_state = 56
			56 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@offices value = 49 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		56 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			if = {
				limit = {
					free_building_slots = {
						building = offices
						size > 0
						include_locked = no
					}
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
				ROOT = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_mutuals_state_56_state_offices }
			}
		}
	}
}

			NOT = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_workers_control }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_pcf_production_speed_arms_factory_factor = 0.1 tooltip = production_speed_arms_factory_factor_tt }
		add_tech_bonus = {
			name = COLDWAR_FRA2_planned_targets_research
			bonus = 0.5
			uses = 1
			category = CAT_industry
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_pcf_production_factory_max_efficiency_factor = 0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_modifier_production_factory_max_efficiency_factor_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_pcf_civilian_factories_productivity = -0.03 tooltip = civilian_factories_productivity_tt }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_planned_targets

COLDWAR_FRA2_build_self_management_state_58_state_industrial_complex = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_self_management_state_58_state_industrial_complex }
			owns_state = 58
			controls_state = 58
			has_full_control_of_state = 58
			58 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@industrial_complex value = 49 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		58 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			if = {
				limit = {
					free_building_slots = {
						building = industrial_complex
						size > 0
						include_locked = no
					}
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = industrial_complex
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
				ROOT = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_self_management_state_58_state_industrial_complex }
			}
		}
	}
}

			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_cid_cooperatives_state_64_state_offices }
			owns_state = 64
			controls_state = 64
			has_full_control_of_state = 64
			64 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@offices value = 49 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		64 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			if = {
				limit = {
					free_building_slots = {
						building = offices
						size > 0
						include_locked = no
					}
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
				ROOT = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_cid_cooperatives_state_64_state_offices }
			}
		}
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA2_is_1970 = yes

				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_fn_state
			}
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_fn_export }
			COLDWAR_FRA2_assets_ready_fn_protection = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.00650 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		COLDWAR_FRA2_build_fn_protection_state_54_state_arms_factory = yes
		COLDWAR_FRA2_build_fn_protection_state_57_state_industrial_complex = yes

			name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fn_protection_research
			bonus = 0.25
			uses = 1
			category = CAT_industry
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_right_civilian_factories_productivity = -0.03 tooltip = civilian_factories_productivity_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_right_research_speed_factor = -0.03 tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_modifier_research_speed_factor_tt }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_fn_protection

	}

COLDWAR_FRA2_complete_technical_colleges = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA2_is_1970 = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_technical_colleges }
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_education
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_sixth_plan
			}
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_mass_universities }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.00250 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_education
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_education
				add_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_higher_education_pressures
			}
		}
		add_tech_bonus = {
			name = COLDWAR_FRA2_technical_colleges_research
			bonus = 0.5
			uses = 2
			category = CAT_industry
		}
		add_tech_bonus = {
			name = COLDWAR_FRA2_technical_colleges_research
			bonus = 0.5
			uses = 1
			category = CAT_electronics
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_economy_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor = 0.06 tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_modifier_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_economy_education_cost_multiplier_modifier = 0.05 tooltip = education_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_recovery_applied_technical_colleges
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_technical_colleges
		COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_complete_nuclear_scale = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA2_is_1970 = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_nuclear_scale }
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			COLDWAR_FRA2_nuclear_construction_ready = yes
			has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_oil
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_energy_review
			}
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_diverse_energy }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.00750 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_tech_bonus = {
			name = COLDWAR_FRA2_nuclear_scale_research
			bonus = 0.5
			uses = 2
			category = CAT_nuclear_reactors
		}
		unlock_decision_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_fund_reactor
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_economy_production_speed_nuclear_reactor_factor = 0.08 tooltip = production_speed_nuclear_reactor_factor_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_economy_nuclear_energy_generation_modifier = 0.06 tooltip = nuclear_energy_generation_modifier_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_economy_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier = 0.04 tooltip = bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_oil
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_oil
				add_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_oil_dependence_intermediate
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_recovery_applied_nuclear_scale
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_nuclear_scale
		COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

			has_tech = internet_1

				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_national_computers
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_open_computers
			}
			COLDWAR_FRA2_assets_ready_telecoms = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.00450 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		COLDWAR_FRA2_build_telecoms_state_56_state_internet_station = yes
		COLDWAR_FRA2_build_telecoms_state_62_state_internet_station = yes
		add_tech_bonus = {
			name = COLDWAR_FRA2_telecoms_research
			bonus = 0.5
			uses = 1
			category = CAT_electronics
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_telecoms
		COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_conversion_state_54_state_industrial_complex }
			owns_state = 54
			controls_state = 54
			has_full_control_of_state = 54
			54 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@industrial_complex value = 49 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		54 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			if = {
				limit = {
					free_building_slots = {
						building = industrial_complex
						size > 0
						include_locked = no
					}
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = industrial_complex
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
				ROOT = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_conversion_state_54_state_industrial_complex }
			}
		}
	}
}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.00200 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		air_experience = 25
		add_doctrine_cost_reduction = {
			cost_reduction = 0.25
			uses = 1
			category = air_doctrine
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_defence_air_accidents_factor = -0.08 tooltip = air_accidents_factor_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_defence_airforce_personnel_cost_multiplier_modifier = 0.05 tooltip = airforce_personnel_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_earned_flight_schools
		COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_build_nato_land_priority_state_54_state_arms_factory = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_nato_land_priority_state_54_state_arms_factory }
			owns_state = 54
			controls_state = 54
			has_full_control_of_state = 54
			54 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@arms_factory value = 34 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		54 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			if = {
				limit = {
					free_building_slots = {
						building = arms_factory
						size > 0
						include_locked = no
					}
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = arms_factory
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
				ROOT = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_nato_land_priority_state_54_state_arms_factory }
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA2_is_1970 = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_initialized }
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_presidency
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_grenelle
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_regions
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_oil
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_education
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_deterrent
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_postcolonial
			add_timed_idea = {
				idea = COLDWAR_FRA2_franc_stabilisation
				days = 270
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_initialized
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_programmes = yes
		COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_treaties = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_build_fund_reactor_state_59_state_nuclear_reactor = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_fund_reactor_state_59_state_nuclear_reactor }
			owns_state = 59
			controls_state = 59
			has_full_control_of_state = 59
			59 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@nuclear_reactor value = 19 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		59 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = nuclear_reactor
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
			ROOT = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_fund_reactor_state_59_state_nuclear_reactor }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_build_participation_workshops_state_62_state_offices = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_participation_workshops_state_62_state_offices }
			owns_state = 62
			controls_state = 62
			has_full_control_of_state = 62
			62 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@offices value = 49 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		62 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			if = {
				limit = {
					free_building_slots = {
						building = offices
						size > 0
						include_locked = no
					}
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
				ROOT = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_asset_participation_workshops_state_62_state_offices }
			}
		}
	}
}
